## Summary

Each family below lets a user act through an ECDSA signature and also by a direct call. A user whose account has code loses the signed path only. 974 families, 603,257 addresses, $5.8B held. Another 15,275 small template copies of the same shape are not listed.

## Evidence

Each family's recovery sites were read, judged from bytecode or placed by a measured rule; 263 families were also replayed on real transactions.
